I guess so. When it gets like that... for weeks, I might only sleep two or three hours a night. \n\nI'll just wake up way before dawn, and my head's already back in the garage. I don't know, it just... feels like trying to sleep any more is a waste of time.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever hear that man's voice when you're not in one of those high-energy periods?\n\nPatient: Yeah. So... yes.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period lasting at least two weeks when you felt depressed most of the day nearly every day?\n\nPatient: No. I haven't really felt like that.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy periods, do other people tell you that you’re talking much more than usual or that they can’t interrupt you?\n\nPatient: I guess. Um... sometimes when I try to explain my plans, or just... what's been happening, the words just pour out really fast. My husband tells me to slow down. The guys at the shop do too. But... it's just hard to stop. It feels almost impossible once I get going.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy times, do you feel unusually powerful or especially important?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't know about powerful. I just... I was sure I was the only one who really understood where the repair business was actually headed. Like... if everybody just listened to me, I could turn our little shop into the top EV place in the state. I haven't been in a hospital.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy periods, did they ever cause serious problems at work, at home, or with the police?\n\nPatient: I mean... I guess. And... at home, things got pretty tense. Um... I wasn't really keeping track of the days.\n\nDoctor: Thinking of your longest high-energy period, did it last at least 7 days in a row?\n\nPatient: I... I really couldn't tell you. Maybe. I just... I wasn't counting.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy periods, did you feel unusually euphoric or excessively cheerful?\n\nPatient: I guess... yeah. Sometimes I'd feel like... like I could fix every car in town. Just really up. But then... if my husband asked about the dishes, or if one of the other techs moved my socket set... I'd just blow up.
